Method: 提出三组件协同框架：(1) 潜在3D几何感知模块，将中间视觉特征与3D基础模型对齐，获取低级几何线索；(2) 在线3D推理蒸馏模块，通过共享推理锚点token将教师推理提示中的高级空间思维迁移到学生动作提示；(3) 空间增强动作整合模块，将解耦的几何和推理特征联合注入动作查询token。训练后仅保留轻量适配器，丢弃3D基础模型和教师分支。

Result: 在LIBERO、LIBERO-PLUS、SimplerEnv及真实世界操作任务上均达到SOTA性能，且避免了预训练VLM的灾难性遗忘。

Conclusion: 3D几何感知与3D空间推理可以解耦并分层注入，通过隐式协同训练在不增加推理负担的前提下有效提升VLA模型的3D空间推理能力，验证了该框架的有效性和泛化性。

Abstract: We propose a 3D-thinking-guided co-training framework that enables vision-language-action (VLA) models to perform 3D spatial reasoning implicitly during action prediction. Our core insight is that 3D geometry perception and 3D spatial reasoning are distinct capabilities that can be disentangled and injected at different feature hierarchies. During training, three tightly coupled components work in concert primarily within the latent space: (1) To gain geometric priors, a latent 3D geometry perception module aligns intermediate visual features with a 3D foundation model, acquiring low-level geometric cues without architectural modifications to the VLM backbone. (2) Complementing this, an online 3D reasoning distillation module mitigates the prompt-induced reasoning gap via a shared reasoning anchor token. During 3D VLM co-training, this anchor is emitted as the first output token to robustly encode spatial priors. During VLA training, it serves as an input token inserted between the task and action instructions, transferring high-level spatial thinking from explicit teacher reasoning prompts to student action prompts without chain-of-thought text generation. (3) These disentangled geometric and reasoning features are then united by a spatially augmented action integration, which jointly injects them into the action-query tokens as hierarchical spatial conditions to prevent action shortcuts. At deployment, our method retains only its lightweight adapters to perform implicit 3D reasoning, discarding the 3D foundation model and the teacher branch used for supervision. Consequently, it operates purely on 2D images without 3D sensors, external models, or explicit text generation while preventing catastrophic forgetting of the pretrained VLM, achieving state-of-the-art performance on LIBERO, LIBERO-PLUS, SimplerEnv, and real-world manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [38] [INTACT: Ego-Guided Typed Sparse Evidence Retrieval for Heterogeneous Collaborative Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04437)
*Chen Li,Shengrong Yuan,Jialong Zuo,Xinzhong Zhu,Nong Sang,Changxin Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: INTACT提出了一种以自我为中心的引导式类型化稀疏证据检索框架，用于异构协同感知。该框架通过让自我车辆发出类型化证据查询，协作车辆仅在查询位置响应局部证据，从而实现零训练异构插入协议。在OPV2V-H上达到80.1 AP70，仅需0.52M额外参数和18.0 log2通信量（相比密集特征传输压缩约16倍），在DAIR-V2X上达到43.8 AP50。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的异构协作感知方法遵循"翻译优先"范式，需要将协作方的特征对齐、适配或投影到自我兼容空间，这导致部署与协作方特定的适配耦合，新加入的异构代理集成成本高昂。

Method: INTACT框架包含三个核心设计：(1) 自我车辆发出类型化证据查询，表达可疑对象和证据不足的区域；(2) 协作方仅在查询位置响应局部证据；(3) 自我通过稀疏每查询路由选择有用响应，并通过门控残差回写注入。兼容性要求从全局特征图可解释性降低为局部类型化响应可比性，支持零训练异构插入。

Result: 在OPV2V-H上，INTACT达到80.1 AP70，仅需0.52M额外参数和18.0 log2通信量（约16倍压缩）；在DAIR-V2X真实世界条件下达到43.8 AP50。

Conclusion: INTACT通过改变兼容性要求范式，从全局特征图解释性转变为局部类型化响应可比性，实现了高效的异构协作感知和零训练异构插入协议，在模拟和真实世界基准上均验证了有效性和可部署性。

Abstract: Collaborative perception extends the perceptual range of autonomous vehicles by sharing information across agents, but heterogeneous sensors and perception models make intermediate feature fusion difficult to deploy at scale. Existing heterogeneous collaboration methods typically follow a translation-first paradigm: collaborator features must be aligned, adapted, or projected into an ego-compatible space before fusion. Such feature-compatibility contracts improve fixed-system performance, but they couple deployment to collaborator-specific adaptation and make newly joined heterogeneous agents costly to integrate. To address this gap, we propose INTACT, an ego-guided typed sparse evidence retrieval framework for heterogeneous collaborative perception. Instead of translating an entire collaborator feature map, INTACT lets the ego vehicle issue typed evidence queries that express suspected objects and evidence-deficient regions. Collaborators respond only with local evidence at queried locations, and the ego selects useful responses through sparse per-query routing and injects them through gated residual write-back. This changes the compatibility requirement from global feature-map interpretability to local, typed response comparability under ego-issued queries, enabling a zero-training heterogeneous insertion protocol in which the ego interface is trained once and new collaborators join through checkpoint merging. Extensive experiments on simulated and real-world heterogeneous collaborative perception benchmarks validate the effectiveness and deployability of INTACT. On OPV2V-H, INTACT achieves 80.1 AP70 with only 0.52M additional parameters and 18.0 $\log_2$ communication volume, corresponding to about 16$\times$ compression over dense feature transmission. On DAIR-V2X, INTACT achieves 43.8 AP50 under challenging real-world conditions.

</details>


### [39] [Radiomic Feature Selection Using Gradient Loss of Deep Neural Network for Lung Cancer Stage Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04453)
*Hina Shakir,Mohammad Mohatram,Javeed Hussain,Syed Rizwan Ali,Muhammad Irfan Memon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种梯度损失递归特征消除（GL-RFE）框架，利用深度神经网络的梯度敏感性分析从106个影像组学特征中选择最关键的15个特征，用于肺癌分期检测，在测试集上实现了约90%的准确率、精确率、召回率和F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 影像组学数据集通常具有高维度、小样本的特点，特征选择对于构建可靠的预测模型至关重要。现有特征选择方法难以有效捕捉非线性特征交互，因此需要一种更有效的方法来识别最具影响力的影像组学特征。

Method: GL-RFE框架：通过计算深度神经网络损失相对于输入特征的梯度来评估特征重要性，递归消除贡献最小的特征，最终保留前15个关键特征用于肺癌早期与晚期分期的分类任务。

Result: 在测试集上，准确率90.22%、精确率90.10%、召回率90.24%、F1分数90.16%。可视化分析（相关性热力图、分布图）证实特征冗余降低、类别可分性提升，且优于传统特征选择方法。

Conclusion: GL-RFE能有效捕捉非线性特征交互，增强模型泛化能力，提供了一种可复现、可解释的影像组学癌症分期检测方法，适用于高维小样本生物医学数据集，并有望推广至基因组学、多模态临床分析等领域。

Abstract: Radiomics enables extraction of quantitative imaging biomarkers from medical images and has become an important tool for computer-aided cancer diagnosis. However, radiomics datasets are typically high-dimensional with limited samples, making feature selection a critical step for building reliable predictive models. This study proposes a Gradient-Loss Recursive Feature Elimination (GL-RFE) framework that integrates gradient sensitivity analysis from a deep neural network to identify the most influential radiomic features for lung cancer stage detection. A total of 106 radiomic features were extracted from chest Computed Tomography (CT) scans using the PyRadiomics extension of the 3D Slicer platform. The proposed method evaluates feature importance by computing gradients of the network loss with respect to input features and recursively eliminates features with minimal contribution. The resulting top-15 radiomic features are used to train a deep neural network classifier for distinguishing early-stage and advanced-stage lung cancer. The proposed framework achieves strong classification performance, with accuracy of 90.22%, precision of 90.10%, recall of 90.24%, and F1-score of 90.16% on the test dataset. Visualization analyses, including correlation heat maps and distribution plots, further confirm reduced feature redundancy and improved class separability. Compared to conventional feature selection techniques, GL-RFE effectively captures nonlinear feature interactions and enhances model generalization. The presented protocol provides a reproducible and interpretable methodology for radiomics-based cancer stage detection and is particularly suitable for high-dimensional, small-sample biomedical datasets, with potential applications in other domains such as genomics and multimodal clinical analysis.

</details>


### [40] [Imagine Before You Draw: Visual Prompt Engineering for Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04457)
*Liyu Jia,Fengda Zhang,Jiachun Pan,Kesen Zhao,Saining Zhang,Wang Lin,Weijia Wu,Yue Liao,Aojun Zhou,Hanwang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出视觉提示工程（VPE），通过内部架构中先自回归生成视觉语义token（如SigLIP 2）作为中间语义规划，再生成完整图像token，从而在不引入信息瓶颈的情况下弥合文本到像素的建模差距，在类条件生成、文生图和图像编辑任务上均取得更好效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在两难：外部双阶段pipeline（如X-Omni、BLIP3o-Next）引入信息瓶颈，扩散解码器无法联合访问原始输入和语义规划，导致细节保留能力差；内部架构（如Transfusion、BAGEL、Show-o2）虽支持跨模态交互，但缺乏中间语义引导，仍面临文本到像素的建模鸿沟。

Method: 提出视觉提示工程（VPE），无缝集成到内部架构中。模型先自回归生成视觉语义token（如SigLIP 2）作为"视觉提示"捕获语义布局，再基于该规划生成完整图像token。整个过程在一个模型内部完成，解码器可联合访问原始输入和语义规划。

Result: VPE在类条件生成、文生图和图像编辑任务上均有效。在编辑保真度上显著优于外部pipeline方法（PSNR: 26.76 vs. 19.92），同时保持竞争性的编辑响应速度。VPE还能加速训练收敛并提升生成质量上限。

Conclusion: VPE通过内部集成的方式融合语义规划与图像生成，有效避免了外部pipeline的信息瓶颈问题，在提升生成质量的同时大幅增强了编辑任务的细节保留能力，为视觉语义引导的生成模型提供了更优的设计范式。

Abstract: Incorporating visual semantic representations as an intermediate step before image generation can reduce the modeling difficulty between text and images, thereby improving generation quality. Recent works such as X-Omni and BLIP3o-Next have explored this direction, but they typically use a two-stage external pipeline: a separate autoregressive model first generates semantic tokens, which are then fed as conditioning to an independent diffusion decoder. Since the decoder cannot jointly access the original input and the semantic plan, this design introduces an information bottleneck that limits detail preservation in downstream tasks such as editing. Internal architectures such as Transfusion, BAGEL, and Show-o2 avoid this bottleneck by enabling cross-modal interaction within a single model, but they still face the difficult text-to-pixel modeling gap without intermediate semantic guidance. We propose Visual Prompt Engineering (VPE), which can be seamlessly integrated into such internal frameworks. Specifically, the model first autoregressively generates visual semantic tokens (e.g., SigLIP 2) as "visual prompts" that capture the semantic layout, then generates the full image tokens conditioned on this plan. We validate VPE across class-conditional generation, text-to-image generation, and image editing, covering various token types and model architectures. Results show that VPE can accelerate convergence, raise quality ceilings, and through internal integration, achieve substantially better editing preservation (PSNR: 26.76 vs. 19.92) than external alternatives of the same parameter scale, while maintaining competitive editing responsiveness.

</details>


### [41] [ChannelTok: Efficient Flexible-Length Vision Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04461)
*Sukriti Paul,Arpit Bansal,Tom Goldstein*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于通道维度的灵活长度视觉分词器（ChannelTok），通过将每个潜在通道视为视觉token，结合参数高效的CNN-Transformer混合骨干网络和随机尾部丢弃训练策略，在显著降低计算成本和模型大小的同时，实现了与SOTA方法相当的感知质量（rFID 2.92），解码速度提升8.6倍，参数量减少2.1倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵活视觉分词器（flexible vision tokenizers）虽然取得了SOTA质量，但依赖于参数量庞大的骨干网络和缓慢的多步生成解码器，计算成本极高。本文旨在打破这一困境，设计一种简单、轻量且快速的通道级灵活长度分词器，在保持高质量的同时大幅提升效率。

Method: 1) **通道级Token化**：将每个潜在通道视为一个视觉token，摒弃传统的空间token范式。2) **CNN-Transformer混合骨干**：采用参数高效的混合架构。3) **随机尾部丢弃训练**：训练过程中随机丢弃尾部通道，迫使通道按语义重要性自然排序。4) **灵活推理**：推理时仅保留前k个通道即可实现灵活压缩，并支持变长自回归图像生成。

Result: 在ImageNet上的实验表明，该方法在不同token预算下均保持一致性质量。模型在rFID 2.92的感知质量下，解码速度比次优方案快8.6倍，参数量仅159M，比次优方案小2.1倍，建立了新的质量-效率前沿。

Conclusion: 通道级分词化是一种强大且实用的视觉表示范式，为高效视觉表示提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Leading flexible vision tokenizers achieve SOTA quality at an extreme cost, relying on parameter-heavy backbones and slow, multi-step generative decoders. We depart from this complex, spatial-token paradigm and introduce a simple, lightweight, and fast channel-wise flexible-length tokenizer. Our method treats each latent channel as a visual token, enabling a parameter-efficient CNN-Transformer hybrid backbone. Furthermore, employing a stochastic tail-dropping paradigm during training naturally forces channels to organize by semantic importance. This allows for flexible compression at inference by simply retaining the first $k$ channels, and naturally enables variable-length autoregressive image generation. We validate our approach through extensive experiments on ImageNet, demonstrating consistent quality across diverse token budgets. The results establish a new quality-efficiency frontier: our model achieves state-of-the-art perceptual quality (rFID 2.92) while being $8.6\times$ faster in decoding and $2.1\times$ smaller (159M params) than the next-best alternative. Our work establishes channel-wise tokenization as a powerful and practical paradigm for efficient visual representation. Project page: https://channeltok.github.io

</details>


### [42] [Adaptive Calibration for Fair and Performant Facial Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04469)
*Ryan Brown,Chris Russell*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出自适应校准(AC)策略，通过将归一化嵌入的余弦相似度映射到校准概率，并融入局部上下文信息，解决同一余弦距离在不同嵌入区域对应不同匹配概率的根本性不匹配问题，在不使用人口统计元数据的情况下提升人脸识别的整体性能和公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人脸识别中，余弦相似度存在根本性不匹配问题——相同的距离值在不同嵌入区域可能对应不同的匹配概率，导致校准不准确且存在公平性问题。现有校准方法要么忽略局部上下文，要么需要人口统计分组标签，且常常以牺牲某些群体性能为代价换取公平性。

Method: 将归一化嵌入的余弦相似度映射到良好校准的概率，通过融入局部上下文信息进行区域特定的连续校准，无需人口统计元数据。

Result: 在多种预训练模型和标准基准上，在准确率和公平性指标上均一致优于现有方法。

Conclusion: AC提供了一种实用且公平的人脸识别校准方案，无需人口统计分组标注，在提升整体性能的同时避免了为追求公平性而"降级"某些群体性能的问题。

Abstract: We introduce Adaptive Calibration (AC), a novel calibration strategy for facial recognition that maps cosine similarity between normalized embeddings to well-calibrated probabilities. By incorporating local context into calibration, Adaptive Calibration corrects for a fundamental mismatch in cosine similarity, whereby the same distance can correspond to different match probabilities in different embedding regions. Our approach improves both overall performance and results in a fairer calibration without requiring demographic metadata.
  Our approach consistently dominates existing methods both on accuracy and fairness metrics across a variety of pretrained models and standard benchmarks. AC provides a practical solution for equitable facial recognition, without requiring demographic group annotations, and while improving overall performance. Unlike existing approaches, our method provides continuous, region-specific calibration that avoids "leveling down" where fairness comes at the cost of degraded performance for some groups.

</details>


### [43] [Evaluating Reasoning Fidelity in Visual Text Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04479)
*Jiajun Hong,Jiawei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 当前文本到图像模型虽能生成视觉清晰的文本，但在复杂推理任务中频繁出现语义错误和逻辑不一致，与纯文本模型之间存在显著推理能力差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究T2I模型在需要通过渲染文本表达完整推理过程时，是否真正具备推理保真度，还是仅模仿表面模式。

Method: 通过长文本渲染、事实知识探测、上下文理解和多步推理四个维度，系统评估T2I模型在视觉文本生成中的推理表现。

Result: 当前T2I模型在视觉文本清晰的情况下，仍频繁产生语义错误、逻辑不一致和错误中间步骤，推理能力显著弱于纯文本模型。

Conclusion: 视觉文本生成与程序化推理之间存在巨大鸿沟，亟需更可靠的视觉文本推理方法。

Abstract: Recent text-to-image (T2I) models can render highly legible and well-structured text within images, enabling applications including document generation and slide generation. However, it remains unclear whether such systems faithfully preserve reasoning ability when complex solutions must be expressed directly through rendered text, or whether they merely imitate surface-level patterns. We investigate this question by evaluating reasoning fidelity in visual text generation, where models must express complete reasoning processes as images. Our evaluation includes long text rendering, factual knowledge probing, context understanding, and multi-step reasoning. Across these settings, we find that current T2I models frequently produce semantic errors, logical inconsistencies, and incorrect intermediate steps, even when the rendered text appears visually clear. These failures contrast with the strong reasoning performance of text-only models on the same tasks. Our findings reveal a substantial gap between visual text generation and procedural reasoning, motivating more reliable visual text reasoning.

</details>


### [44] [IMPose: Interactive Multi-person Pose Estimation with Dynamic Correction Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04480)
*Haoyang Ge,Jian Ma,Ziwen Wang,Qihe Wang,Jianqi Fan,Hongzhi Yu,Xingyu Chen,Kun Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: IMPose 是一个交互式多人动态姿态标注工具，通过双级跟踪机制（关键点级和实例级）将稀疏人工修正传播为密集连贯的姿势轨迹，显著减少重复性手动标注工作，在低点击设置下实现了精度与效率的良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量动态人体姿态标注对实现精确的运动感知至关重要，但现有工具要么缺乏时序修正传播能力，要么无法处理多人场景，导致过多的人工干预。本文旨在解决这些局限，提出高效的交互式多人动态姿态标注方案。

Method: IMPose 提出双级跟踪机制：(1) 关键点级：通过序列建模确保单帧修正的时序传播；(2) 实例级：采用关键点感知嵌入与相对位置编码维持多人跨帧一致性。此外，轨迹库存储历史姿态和实例线索，增强长距离时序关联，在遮挡和运动模糊等挑战性场景下稳定标注。

Result: 在 3DPW 上，1,050 帧视频仅需 27 次点击；在 PoseTrack21 上，每个 84 帧的轨迹片段仅需 3 次点击。扩展后的 PoseTrack21 包含 188K 姿态实例（355 万个关键点），仅需 10 名标注员 10 小时即可完成。

Conclusion: IMPose 通过将稀疏人工修正高效转化为密集、连贯的姿态轨迹，大幅减少了逐帧重复人工精调，在不同交互预算下均实现了强鲁棒的精度-效率权衡，特别在低点击标注场景中优势显著。

Abstract: High-quality dynamic human pose annotation equips AI with precise motion kinematics to enable human behavior mastery, yet remains labor-intensive and time-consuming. Current annotation tools either lack temporal correction propagation or fail in multi-person scenarios, necessitating excessive manual intervention. In this paper, we introduce IMPose, an interactive tool for multi-person dynamic pose annotation. It features a dual-level tracking mechanism that propagates one-frame multi-person pose corrections from annotators across entire videos. The keypoint-level ensures corrections temporal propagation via sequential modeling, while the instance-level employs keypoint-aware embedding with relative positional encoding to maintain multi-person cross-frame consistency. To further improve robustness, IMPose maintains historical pose and instance cues in a trajectory bank, which enhances long-range temporal association and stabilizes annotation in challenging cases such as occlusion and motion blur. By converting sparse human corrections into dense and coherent pose trajectories, our framework significantly reduces repeated manual refinement across frames. Extensive experiments show that IMPose consistently achieves a strong accuracy efficiency trade off under different interaction budgets, demonstrating particular advantages in low click annotation settings. IMPose achieves high precision annotation with high efficiency, requiring only 27 clicks per 1,050 frame video on 3DPW and 3 clicks per tracklet per 84-frame on PoseTrack21. We further expand PoseTrack21 with 188K pose instances (3.55M keypoints) at a minimal cost of 10 annotators in 10 hours. The annotation tool, codes, and extended dataset will be open-sourced.

</details>


### [45] [SpurAudio: A Benchmark for Studying Shortcut Learning in Few-Shot Audio Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13672)
*Giries Abu Ayoub,Morad Tukan,Loay Mualem*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SpurAudio基准，揭示少样本音频分类中模型容易利用前景与背景环境之间的虚假相关性，即使在大型预训练音频基础模型中也存在此脆弱性，且不同方法对虚假相关性的敏感度存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有少样本分类评估隐含假设目标概念独立于上下文线索，但真实场景中模型可利用前景-背景间的虚假相关性。这种效应在图像领域已有研究，但在音频分类中尚未被探索，且现有音频基准缺乏对上下文结构的可控评估。

Method: 利用音频中前景事件与背景环境天然可分离的特性，构建SpurAudio基准，实现对支持集与查询集之间上下文偏移的多层级可控评估。

Result: 1) 许多最先进的少样本方法在背景相关性被破坏时性能严重下降，尽管在标准评估下表现相近；2) 大型预训练音频基础模型同样存在此脆弱性；3) 在传统基准下表现相似的方法对虚假相关性的敏感度差异显著。

Conclusion: 少样本音频分类模型普遍存在利用背景虚假相关性的问题，需要设计能显式探测上下文依赖性的基准来评估FSC模型。

Abstract: Few-shot classification (FSC) is widely used for learning from limited labeled data, yet most evaluations implicitly assume that target concepts are independent of contextual cues. In real-world settings, however, examples often appear within rich contexts, allowing models to exploit spurious correlations between foreground content and background signals. While such effects have been studied in few-shot image classification, their role in few-shot audio classification remains largely unexplored, and existing audio benchmarks offer limited control over contextual structure. We introduce SpurAudio, a benchmark that leverages the natural separability of foreground events and background environments in audio to enable controlled, multi-level evaluation of contextual shifts across support and query sets. Using this benchmark, we show that many state-of-the-art few-shot methods suffer severe performance degradation when background correlations are disrupted, despite achieving similar accuracy under standard evaluation protocols. Crucially, this vulnerability persists even in large pretrained audio foundation models, ruling out limited backbone capacity as an explanation. Moreover, methods that appear comparable under conventional benchmarks can exhibit markedly different sensitivity to spurious correlations, revealing systematic algorithmic strengths and vulnerabilities tied to how feature representations interact with classifier heads at inference time. These findings provide new insight into the behavior of few-shot methods in audio and highlight the need for benchmarks that explicitly probe context dependence when evaluating FSC models.

</details>


### [46] [SFMambaNet: Spectral-Frequency Enhanced Selective State Space Model for Correspondence Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04493)
*Zhihua Wang,Yanping Li,Yizhang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SFMambaNet，一种融合频域感知的Mamba-based两视图对应关系筛选网络，通过局部谱-几何注意力块和谱集成全局Mamba块协同增强内外点区分能力，在多项挑战性任务上超越当前最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN-based方法依赖粗几何特征，难以捕捉内点间的细微几何一致性；Mamba-based方法虽具备全局感受野和长序列建模能力，但在隐状态空间中容易积累不一致特征，导致内外点难以区分。

Method: 提出SFMambaNet，包含两个协同组件：1) 局部谱-几何注意力(LSGA)块，将谱位置编码融入局部图交互，并引入多尺度Mamba处理以增强细微几何一致性捕捉；2) 谱集成全局Mamba(SIGM)块，在状态空间中嵌入频率门控机制，利用LSGA提供的频率信息显式抑制高频噪声积累和模糊特征传播，实现近线性复杂度的鲁棒全局上下文建模。

Result: 在多项挑战性任务上的大量实验表明，SFMambaNet优于当前最先进方法。

Conclusion: 首次将频域感知引入对应关系筛选任务，通过谱-频增强的Mamba架构有效提升了内外点可分性和全局建模能力，验证了频域信息在该任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Correspondence pruning aims to identify inliers from an initial set of correspondences. Most existing Graph Neural Network (GNN)-based methods rely on geometric features mapped from coarse Euclidean coordinates, which struggle to capture the subtle geometric consistencies presented by inliers. While Mamba-based methods possess global receptive fields and long sequence modeling capabilities, they tend to accumulate substantial inconsistent features within the hidden state space, making it difficult to distinguish inliers from outliers. In this paper, we integrate frequency domain perception into this task for the first time and propose SFMambaNet, a novel Spectral-Frequency enhanced Mamba-based two-view correspondence pruning network. Our method is collaboratively composed of two components: First, we design a Local Spectral-Geometric Attention (LSGA) block. LSGA incorporates spectral positional encoding into local graph interactions and introduces multi-scale Mamba processing to enhance the capture of subtle geometric consistencies and improve local feature discriminability. Building upon this, we design a Spectral-Integrated Global Mamba (SIGM) block. SIGM embeds a frequency gating mechanism within the state space, utilizing the frequency information provided by LSGA to explicitly suppress high-frequency noise accumulation within hidden states and mitigate the propagation of inconsistent features. This enhances inlier-outlier separability and achieves robust global context modeling capabilities with nearly linear complexity. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SFMambaNet outperforms current state-of-the-art methods on several challenging tasks. The code is available at https://github.com/Kirito14IT/SFMambaNet.

</details>


### [47] [Optical-Guided Neural Collapse for SAR Few-Shot Class Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04528)
*Fan Zhang,Sijin Zheng,Fei Ma,Qiang Yin,Yongsheng Zhou,Fei Gao,Xian Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对SAR图像的小样本增量学习（FSCIL）中数据稀缺、方位角敏感性导致类内差异大、类间混淆以及灾难性遗忘问题，本文提出一种光学引导的SAR FSCIL框架，利用光学ATR数据集提取正交特征子空间作为几何先验，通过主角约束投影和冻结simplex-ETF几何的分类器损失，联合诱导神经坍缩（neural collapse），在24类SAR基准上取得最高最终精度和良好的性能-退化权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAR图像的FSCIL面临三个核心挑战：(1) SAR数据极度稀缺；(2) 强方位角敏感性导致大的类内变化和类间混淆；(3) 序列化增量更新引发对旧类的灾难性遗忘。现有方法未能有效解决这些问题，因此需要利用数据丰富的光学ATR数据集的知识来引导SAR特征学习。

Method: (1) 从光学ATR数据集中提取正交特征子空间，作为几何先验；(2) 通过主角约束将SAR特征投影到这些正交子空间上，实现从光学域到SAR域的判别性结构迁移；(3) 设计投影损失和分类器损失，后者利用冻结的simplex-ETF几何进行优化；(4) 上述损失联合诱导神经坍缩：使特征围绕类均值聚集（类内紧凑性），同时保持大类间角度（类间可分离性）。

Result: 在包含光学ATR数据集和24类SAR ATR数据集的基准（基础训练+7个增量会话）上，与NCFSCIL等最新FSCIL方法相比，本文方法取得了最高最终准确率，并在最终性能与性能退化之间实现了良好权衡。神经坍缩指标显示改进的类内紧凑性和类间可分离性，学习特征更接近理想的simplex-ETF几何。

Conclusion: 本文验证了利用光学域正交子空间几何先验引导SAR FSCIL学习的有效性，神经坍缩机制能够有效缓解SAR数据稀缺和方位角敏感性问题，为跨模态小样本增量学习提供了新思路。

Abstract: Few-shot class-incremental learning (FSCIL) in synthetic aperture radar imagery presents unique challenges due to severe data scarcity and SAR-specific variability. In particular, strong azimuth sensitivity in SAR induces large intra-class variation and inter-class confusion, and FSCIL sequential updates further lead to catastrophic forgetting of previously learned classes. Inspired by neural collapse, we propose an optical-guided SAR FSCIL framework, which derives orthogonal feature subspaces from a data-rich optical ATR dataset and uses them as geometric priors to guide SAR feature learning. SAR features are projected onto these orthogonal subspaces via principal angle constraints, effectively transferring discriminative structure from the optical to the SAR domain. Specifically, our projection loss and the classifier loss optimized with a frozen simplex-ETF geometry jointly induce neural collapse by concentrating features around class means while maintaining large inter-class angles. We evaluate the approach on a benchmark comprising an optical ATR dataset and a SAR ATR dataset with 24 target classes, organized into a base training session and seven incremental sessions. Compared with recent FSCIL methods including NCFSCIL and so on, our method achieves the highest final accuracy and a favorable trade-off between final performance and performance degradation. Moreover, neural collapse metrics show improved intra-class compactness and inter-class separability, indicating that the learned features more closely approximate the ideal simplex-ETF geometry.

</details>


### [48] [Impostor: An Agent-Curated Benchmark for Realistic AIGC Manipulation Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04545)
*Zhenliang Li,Yutao Hu,Qixiong Wang,Wenpeng Du,Hongxiang Jiang,Jiasong Wu,Xiaolong Jiang,Jungong Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了Impostor高质量AI编辑图像篡改定位数据集（含10万张图像，覆盖7种AIGC模型、3种篡改类型、多篡改区域），配套CraftAgent闭环自动生成框架，以及PhaseAware-Net（PANet）语义-取证一致性学习框架用于定位语义合理但取证异常的区域。实验表明Impostor对现有大模型和专用方法构成挑战，PANet在多个基准上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像篡改检测与定位（IMDL）基准在视觉真实性、篡改多样性和生成器覆盖方面存在局限，难以反映近期生成式图像编辑技术的发展趋势。

Method: 1) CraftAgent闭环框架：场景感知→编辑规划→篡改执行→质量验证→迭代反思，自动生成多样化真实篡改图像；2) Impostor数据集：10万张篡改图像，7种AIGC模型，3种篡改类型；3) PANet：局部相位建模+语义-取证一致性学习，定位语义合理但取证异常区域。

Result: Impostor对现有大视觉语言模型（LVLMs）和专用IMDL方法构成显著挑战；PANet在Impostor及多个公开基准上取得最优性能。

Conclusion: 所提数据集和方法有效弥补了现有IMDL基准的不足，为AIGC时代图像篡改检测提供了更全面的评估基准和更有效的检测框架。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative image editing have improved the realism and controllability of localized image manipulation, raising new challenges for image manipulation detection and localization (IMDL). However, existing IMDL benchmarks still have limitations in visual realism, manipulation diversity, and generator coverage, making it difficult to reflect recent trends in image manipulation. To address these limitations, we introduce Impostor, a high-quality AI-edited image manipulation localization dataset containing 100K manipulated images. Impostor is constructed by CraftAgent, a closed-loop agent framework that integrates scene perception, editing planning, manipulation execution, quality validation, and iterative reflection to automatically generate diverse and visually realistic manipulated images. Moreover, Impostor contains images generated by seven recent AIGC models across three manipulation types and includes multiple manipulated regions, providing a more comprehensive benchmark for AIGC-based IMDL. Furthermore, we propose PhaseAware-Net (PANet), a semantic-forensic framework that introduces local phase modeling and semantic-forensic consistency learning to better localize semantically plausible yet forensically disrupted manipulated regions. Extensive experiments show that Impostor poses significant challenges to existing large vision-language models (LVLMs) and specialized IMDL methods, while PANet achieves superior performance on Impostor and multiple public benchmarks.

</details>


### [49] [4D Reconstruction from Sparse Dynamic Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04593)
*Kazuki Ozeki,Shun Kenney,Yuto Shibata,Eisuke Takeuchi,Takuya Narihira,Kazumi Fukuda,Ryosuke Sawata,Yuki Mitsufuji,Yoshimitsu Aoki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对稀疏动态相机设置（多台独立移动相机）下的4D重建问题，提出了一种简单有效的3D轨迹初始化方法，结合帧间特征匹配与帧内点跟踪确保时空一致性，并引入噪声鲁棒的深度排序正则化损失和时空多样批次采样策略，同时发布了包含5个序列的LetCamsGo真实世界数据集，实验证明了该方法在动态区域4D重建质量上的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单目动态相机4D重建受限于深度模糊，而密集固定相机方案成本高昂且不实用。稀疏动态相机设置虽具多视图约束且成本低（适用于体育、演唱会等场景），但现有方法无法解决跨视图和跨时间的复杂时空不一致问题，且缺乏标准化基准数据集。

Method: 1) 提出一种3D轨迹初始化方法，通过集成相机间特征匹配（inter-camera feature matching）与相机内点跟踪（intra-camera point tracking）确保时空一致性；2) 引入噪声鲁棒的深度排序正则化损失（noise-robust depth-ordering regularization loss）；3) 提出时空多样批次采样策略（spatiotemporally diverse batch sampling）以增强优化稳定性和跨视图泛化能力；4) 构建LetCamsGo数据集（3台独立移动相机+1台固定相机，5个序列，4种环境）。

Result: 在LetCamsGo数据集上的全面基准测试表明，所提框架在动态区域的4D重建质量上优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 该工作为低成本的野外4D重建开辟了新范式，验证了稀疏动态相机设置在实际视频制作中的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Although dynamic 3D (i.e., 4D) reconstruction from a monocular dynamic camera has recently advanced, it remains fundamentally limited by depth ambiguity. In this paper, we focus on an alternative practical way, i.e., sparse dynamic camera setup, where a handful of independently moving cameras capture the same subjects. While keeping capture costs low, this setup introduces multi-view constraints and remains practical for real-world video production such as sports, concerts, and TV shows. Despite its potential, our experiments show that naive extensions of existing monocular or dense-fixed camera-based methods are insufficient since they fail to resolve the complex spatiotemporal inconsistencies across views and time. To fill this gap, we propose a simple yet effective 3D track initialization method designed to ensure spatiotemporal consistency by integrating inter-camera feature matching with intra-camera point tracking. Additionally, we incorporate a noise-robust depth-ordering regularization loss and a spatiotemporally diverse batch sampling strategy to enhance optimization stability and cross-view generalization. Furthermore, to address the lack of standardized benchmarks for this task, we introduce LetCamsGo, a new real-world video dataset with 5 sequences across 4 diverse environments, recorded by three independently moving cameras and one fixed camera. Comprehensive benchmarking on LetCamsGo demonstrated that our proposed framework improves 4D reconstruction quality in dynamic regions compared with baselines, paving the way for a low-cost 4D reconstruction paradigm in the wild.

</details>


### [50] [COMBINER: Composed Image Retrieval Guided by Attribute-based Neighbor Relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04604)
*Zixu Li,Yupeng Hu,Zhiwei Chen,Haokun Wen,Xuemeng Song,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出COMBINER网络，通过属性原型解决组合图像检索中视觉相似但属性不相关的问题，包含自适应语义解耦、统一原型组合和双重关系建模三个核心模块，在三个基准数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CIR方法忽视了视觉相似但属性不同的样本情况，导致多模态特征融合和相似度建模效果不佳。具体面临三个核心问题：属性级语义纠缠、跨模态不一致性、监督信号缺失。

Method: 提出COMBINER网络框架，包含三个模块：(1) 自适应语义解耦模块，基于多模态原始特征解耦属性特征；(2) 统一原型组合模块，构建跨模态统一原型(CUP)促进多模态特征组合；(3) 双重关系建模模块，基于属性相似度挖掘成对和邻居关系。这是首个处理视觉相似但属性不相关样本的方法。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的全面实验证实了COMBINER的有效性。

Conclusion: COMBINER通过属性原型相似度度量，实现了对样本间语义关系更准确的理解，优于传统的邻居关系建模CIR方法。

Abstract: Composed Image Retrieval (CIR) represents a challenging retrieval task that targets locating specific images through multimodal inputs. Despite recent progress in CIR techniques, prior approaches often overlook cases where images appear visually alike yet differ in attributes, potentially undermining both multimodal feature fusion and similarity modeling. To mitigate this limitation, we design a unified representation of cross-modal features based on attribute prototypes. Nevertheless, the task is far from straightforward, owing to three core issues: (1) entanglement in attribute-level semantics, (2) inconsistency across modalities, and (3) supervised signal missing. To tackle the above obstacles, we introduce a COMposed image retrieval network guided By attrIbute-based NEighbor Relations (COMBINER). Specifically, we first design an Adaptive Semantic Disentanglement module, which is capable of disentangling attribute features based on multimodal primitive features. Secondly, we propose a Unified Prototype-based Composition module, which can construct cross-modal unified prototypes (CUP) and facilitate multimodal feature composition. Finally, we introduce a Dual Relations Modeling module, which can mine pairwise and neighbor relations based on attribute similarity. Compared to traditional neighbor relations modeling CIR methods, COMBINER represents the first study addressing the phenomenon of visually similar but attribute-unrelated samples. It achieves a more accurate understanding of the semantic relations among samples by employing an attribute prototype-based similarity metric. Comprehensive experiments conducted on three benchmark datasets confirm the effectiveness of our proposed COMBINER. The implementation of our method will be accessed at https://github.com/Lee-zixu/COMBINER

</details>


### [51] [Beyond Symmetric Alignment: Spectral Diagnostics of Modality Imbalance in Vision-Language Models in the Medical Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04613)
*Alessandro Gambetti,Qiwei Han,Cláudia Soares,Hong Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出非对称对齐指标SAS（谱对齐分数），用于诊断视觉-语言模型在医学图像-文本数据中的模态信息失衡问题。实验表明医学图像比临床报告保留更丰富的结构信息，SAS在医学领域与检索性能的零标签相关性最强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表征对齐指标是对称的，将双模态信息压缩为单一分数，无法区分哪个模态导致了跨模态性能退化，且缺乏针对医学图像-文本数据的诊断工具。

Method: 提出非对称的SAS指标，将两个模态投影到锚定模态的主特征基上，计算特征值加权的每本征模态相关性，从而得到方向性分数，其差值量化模态信息不平衡度。嵌入评估框架，在15个VLM、自然与医学图像-文本数据集上对比6个对齐指标和双向检索。

Result: 医学图像比配对的临床报告保留更丰富的结构信息，这种方向性不对称性被所有现有对称指标掩盖；SAS在医学领域与检索性能的零标签相关性最强。

Conclusion: SAS可作为实用的临床部署诊断工具，揭示了医学多模态学习中模态信息不对称的关键问题，填补了非对称对齐指标的空白。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) struggle when applied to medical image-text data, yet the tools available to diagnose this failure remain limited. Existing representation alignment metrics are symmetric, collapsing both modalities into a single score and hiding which modality drives cross-modal degradation. We introduce the Spectral Alignment Score (SAS), an asymmetric metric that projects both modalities onto the principal eigenbasis of an anchor modality and computes eigenvalue-weighted per-eigenmode correlations, resulting in directional scores whose difference quantifies modality information imbalance. We embed SAS within a benchmarking framework evaluating 15 VLMs across natural and medical image-text datasets alongside 6 alignment metrics and bidirectional retrieval. Our experiments show that medical images retain richer structural information than their paired clinical reports, a directional asymmetry invisible to all competing metrics, and that SAS achieves the strongest zero-label correlation with retrieval performance in the medical domain, positioning it as a practical diagnostic tool for clinical deployment. Code is available at this URL: https://github.com/iamalegambetti/medical-vlms-assessment.

</details>


### [52] [MeshFlow: Efficient Artistic Mesh Generation via MeshVAE and Flow-based Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04621)
*Weiyu Li,Antoine Toisoul,Tom Monnier,Roman Shapovalov,Rakesh Ranjan,Ping Tan,Andrea Vedaldi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MeshFlow提出了一种基于变分自编码器（VAE）和整流流（Rectified Flow）Transformer的新型3D网格生成方法，相比自回归方法实现18倍加速，同时保持高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归（AR）网格生成器存在两个问题：(1) 推理成本随网格规模呈二次增长，可扩展性差；(2) 顶点坐标离散化引入量化误差。目标是解决这些瓶颈。

Method: (1) 使用对比损失监督的VAE，将连续的顶点位置和离散的连接性共同编码到连续的潜在空间中；(2) 基于整流流（Rectified Flow）的Transformer作为生成器，并行生成所有网格顶点和边。

Result: 生成速度比最快的自回归生成器快18倍，在标准网格生成指标上取得了优异的准确率。

Conclusion: 提出了一种高效、可扩展的3D网格生成范式，通过连续潜在表示和并行生成机制克服了自回归方法的根本缺陷。

Abstract: We present MeshFlow, a new method for generating artist-like 3D meshes. Current mesh generators often adopt Auto-Regressive (AR) next-token prediction, a natural choice given the discrete nature of mesh topology. However, AR methods scale poorly because the inference cost is quadratic in mesh size. They also require discretizing the vertex coordinates, which introduces quantization errors. To address these challenges, we introduce a Variational Autoencoder (VAE) that, supervised with a contrastive loss, represents both continuous vertex positions and discrete connectivity in a continuous latent space. This latent space is significantly more compact than prior token-based mesh representations. We then build a 3D generator based on a Rectified Flow transformer, generating all mesh vertices and edges in parallel. Our model generates meshes 18x faster than the fastest AR generator while also achieving excellent accuracy across standard mesh-generation metrics. Homepage: https://mesh-flow.github.io/, Code: https://github.com/facebookresearch/meshflow

</details>


### [53] [Instance-Level Post Hoc Uncertainty Quantification in Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04656)
*Chongzhe Zhang,Zifan Zeng,Qunli Zhang,Feng Liu,Zheng Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MC-GLM方法，使用拉普拉斯近似和蒙特卡洛采样实现目标检测的不确定性量化，无需重新训练且计算高效，在nuScenes数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶中目标检测是安全关键组件，需要量化边界框预测的不确定性以保障安全，且实际部署要求后验不确定性量化方法无需重新训练。

Method: 采用拉普拉斯近似进行后验不确定性量化，提出蒙特卡洛广义线性模型（MC-GLM），通过蒙特卡洛步骤提供实例级不确定性，采样数量恒定且独立于输出实例数，可并行化。

Result: 在nuScenes数据集上使用CenterPoint检测器验证，所提方法产生的不确定性具有良好的质量。

Conclusion: MC-GLM实现了实例级且近似后验的不确定性量化，采样数恒定可并行化，满足自动驾驶部署的实际需求。

Abstract: Object detection is a safety-critical component of autonomous driving. It is essential to quantify the uncertainty in bounding-box predictions for safety assurance. Post hoc uncertainty quantification without retraining aligns with real-world deployment requirements; therefore, we employ the Laplace approximation. Because instance-level uncertainty is needed, linearized inference methods that require multiple backpropagations are not time-efficient, and sampling-based methods are not fully post hoc. We propose Monte-Carlo generalized linearized model (MC-GLM), which provides instance-level and approximately post hoc uncertainty quantification. The number of samples required in the Monte Carlo step is constant and independent of the number of output instances, so it can be parallelized. Experiments on the nuScenes dataset with the CenterPoint detector validate the effectiveness of our method, and the resulting uncertainties exhibit good quality.

</details>


### [54] [Real-Time Automatic License Plate Recognition Using YOLOv8, SORT Tracking, and Temporal Data Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04684)
*Mirza Muhammad Mobeen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个5阶段端到端ALPR流水线，结合YOLOv8纳米模型、SORT多目标跟踪、EasyOCR和时空插值，解决了复杂交通场景下车牌识别的实时性和准确性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频处理的实时性限制严重制约了自动车牌识别在动态交通监控中的应用，现有方法在光照变化、摄像头扫描角度、车辆高速行驶和物理遮挡等非受控变量下，识别精度低且跟踪路径不连续，导致OCR效果差。

Method: 提出5阶段端到端算法流水线：(1) YOLOv8纳米模型进行车辆检测；(2) SORT算法建立帧间时空关联；(3) 专用YOLOv8检测器定位车牌区域；(4) EasyOCR结合位置语法约束进行字符识别；(5) 离线时空边界框插值机制修复碎片化跟踪路径。

Result: 该架构通过深度学习目标检测、运动学多目标跟踪与几何时序数据插值的平滑过渡，有效提升了复杂环境下车牌识别的连续性和OCR准确率。

Conclusion: 提出的5阶段流水线通过YOLOv8与SORT及离线插值的协同，有效缓解了非受控变量对ALPR系统的负面影响，为动态交通监控场景下高保真车牌识别提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: The real-time hardships of video processing seriously limit the usage of Automatic License Plate Recognition (ALPR) with application in dynamic traffic monitoring settings. High-fidelity recognition of unconstrained variables, e.g. drastic variations in illumination, acute camera scans, high vehicle speeds, and harsh physical concealment, is a problem that often leads to disjointed tracking paths and poor Optical Character Recognition (OCR) rates. In order to mitigate these weaknesses, the study proposes a 5 stage, end-to-end algorithmic pipeline, encompassing a smooth transition between deep learning based object detection, multi-object tracking which is kinematic in nature, and geometry temporal data interpolation. The suggested architecture takes advantage of a very powerful YOLOv8 nano model to localize the vehicle at the first stage and then Simple Online and Realtime Tracking (SORT) algorithm is used to build spatial-temporal links between frames. Another, more specific typology of YOLOv8 object detectors the license plate area, channeling the sliced array to an EasyOCR chain under the limitations of positional syntax verification. More importantly, an offline interpolation mechanism of temporal bounding box is initiated to recast fragmented paths.

</details>


### [55] [MeshWeaver: Sparse-Voxel-Guided Surface Weaving for Autoregressive Mesh Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04688)
*Jiale Xu,Wang Zhao,Ying Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MeshWeaver提出了一种自回归网格生成框架，通过直接预测下一个顶点（而非独立坐标）将网格生成视为表面编织过程。其核心是多层级稀疏体素编码器，从三个互补方式注入几何上下文：体素特征作为顶点表示、交叉注意力引导令牌预测、以及作为结构支架约束生成。该方法实现了18%的最先进压缩比，可生成高达16K面的网格，并在几何保真度上显著优于先前方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归网格生成方法存在两个根本局限：(i) 令牌化效率低，导致长序列难以扩展到高多边形网格；(ii) 缺乏几何感知引导，生成仅依赖全局形状嵌入而非局部表面线索。

Method: 提出MeshWeaver框架，核心是多层级稀疏体素编码器，以三种互补方式注入几何上下文：(1) 提供体素特征作为顶点表示；(2) 通过交叉注意力引导令牌预测；(3) 作为结构支架约束生成围绕输入表面。层级设计支持单解码步内的粗到细顶点预测。

Result: 达到18%的最新最优压缩比，可生成高达16K面的网格，几何保真度显著优于以往方法。

Conclusion: 通过将几何感知嵌入自回归生成过程，MeshWeaver有效克服了传统方法在效率与几何保真度上的瓶颈，为高多边形网格生成提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Autoregressive mesh generation has gained attention by tokenizing meshes into sequences and training models in a language-modeling fashion. However, existing approaches suffer from two fundamental limitations: (i) low tokenization efficiency, which yields long token sequences and prevents scaling to high-poly meshes, and (ii) absence of geometry-aware guidance, as generation is conditioned only on global shape embeddings rather than local surface cues. We introduce MeshWeaver, an autoregressive framework that treats mesh generation as a surface weaving process by directly predicting the next vertex instead of independent coordinates. At its core is a multi-level sparse-voxel encoder that injects geometric context into the generative process in three complementary ways: providing voxel features as vertex representations, guiding token prediction via cross-attention to voxel features, and serving as a structural scaffold that constrains generation around the input surface. Our hierarchical design enables coarse-to-fine vertex prediction in a single decoding step, while tightly coupling the generative model with 3D geometry. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MeshWeaver achieves a state-of-the-art compression ratio of 18%, can generate meshes with up to 16K faces, and significantly improves geometric fidelity over prior approaches.

</details>


### [56] [A New Angle on Bones: Robust Pose Estimation in X-Ray and Ultrasound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04700)
*Ron Keuth,Christoph Großbröhmer,Franziska Halm,Miriam Johann,Anne-Nele Schröder,Ludger Tüshaus,Mattias P. Heinrich,Lasse Hansen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于学习的关键点候选与鲁棒线模型拟合（RANSAC、Hough变换）的自动骨骼姿态估计方法，在三个儿科临床角度测量任务（骨折、DDH）上达到平均误差4.1°-5.51°，优于传统地标法，且代码已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像中骨骼角度测量是常规任务，自动化方法可降低时间成本并提高可重复性；传统最小二乘线模型对异常值敏感，因此需要更鲁棒的拟合策略。

Method: 采用基于学习的关键点候选提案，结合假阳性抑制策略与鲁棒拟合技术（RANSAC、Hough变换）提取轴线参数，替代传统最小二乘线模型。

Result: 在骨折片段评估（X光4.1°、超声5.4°）及DDH髋关节超声评估（5.51°）三个任务中，误差均处于临床观察者变异范围内，且显著优于基于地标的方法。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效解决了异常值敏感问题，在儿科角度估计中达到临床可用精度，代码与标注已公开。

Abstract: Measuring the angle between bone structures is a routine task in medical image analysis and provides a key quantitative parameter for diagnosis and treatment planning. Automated methods can reduce time and cost while improving reproducibility. In this work, we address automatic bone pose estimation using a learning-based point candidate proposal followed by a line model to extract axis parameters. Since conventional line models such as least squares are sensitive to outliers, we incorporate false-positive reduction strategies and robust fitting techniques, such as RANSAC and Hough transforms, to improve robustness. We evaluate our method on three clinically relevant paediatric angle estimation tasks: fracture fragment assessment in radiographs and ultrasound and developmental dysplasia of the hip evaluation in ultrasound using the Graf method. Our approach achieves mean errors of $4.1^\circ$, $5.4^\circ$, and $5.51^\circ$, respectively, not only remaining within the expected clinical observer variability, but also significantly outperforming landmark-based methods. Our code and annotations for fracture angle assessment in radiographs are publicly available on GitHub.

</details>


### [57] [Benchmarking Living-Screen-Native GUI Agents on Short-Video Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04701)
*Jiashu Yao,Heyan Huang,Daiqing Wu,Wangke Chen,Huaxi Ai,Haoyu Wen,Zeming Liu,Yuhang Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文定义了“动态屏幕原生GUI代理”任务，指出传统GUI代理假设屏幕在两动作间静止，但短视频等动态界面违背这一假设。作者构建了LivingScreen基准测试，包含基于浏览器的环境、三层任务套件和联合评估准确率与信息效率的指标。实验发现现有前沿模型均未达到人类的表现水平，主要失败模式为过度观察或观察不足，表明观察控制是未来GUI代理缺失的关键能力维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI代理均假设屏幕在两动作之间是静态的，但现实中的短视频等动态界面内容持续播放，用户需要决定看什么、看多久。这一假设与实际应用场景的差距是本研究的主要推动力。

Method: 1) 形式化定义Living-Screen-Native GUI代理任务；2) 构建LivingScreen基准，包括基于浏览器的忠实环境、三层任务套件以及联合评估准确率和信息效率的指标；3) 在短视频平台上评测多个前沿模型。

Result: 现有前沿模型均未达到人类的成本-准确率表现；主要失败模式是过度观察和观察不足，说明观察控制是当前GUI代理缺失的能力。

Conclusion: 动态界面中的观察控制是未来GUI代理研究需要关注的重要能力维度，现有模型在此方面存在明显不足。

Abstract: GUI agents today assume a static screen, where the world is frozen between two actions. However, real interfaces such as short-video applications violate this assumption, as their content keeps playing, and a competent user must decide what to watch and for how long. We formalize this task as Living-Screen-Native GUI agents and introduce LivingScreen, the first benchmark instantiating it on short-video platforms, with a faithful browser-based environment, a three-tier task suite, and metrics that jointly score accuracy and information efficiency. Evaluating extensive frontier models, we find that none reaches the human cost-accuracy performance, and that their dominant failure mode is over- and under-observation, pointing to observation control as a missing capability axis for future GUI agents. All data and code will be available at https://github.com/BITHLP/LivingScreen.

</details>


### [58] [Enhancing MedSAM with a Lightweight Box Predictor for Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04705)
*Amirhossein Movahedisefat,Amirreza Fateh,Mohammad Reza Mohammadi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个集成轻量级Box Predictor模块的MedSAM增强框架，通过单次点击生成边界框来改进医学图像语义分割，仅增加1.6M参数，在四个跨模态数据集上取得优异Dice分数（0.88-0.98）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像语义分割面临数据稀缺和跨模态高变异性挑战。现有基础模型（如SAM）需要特定适配才能用于医学图像，且点提示作为最自然的用户交互方式，在目标结构不规则或对比度差时提供空间上下文不足，导致分割不可靠。

Method: 在MedSAM架构中集成轻量级Box Predictor模块，该模块利用局部图像嵌入特征从单次用户点击估计近似边界框。采用两阶段训练流程：先独立训练Box Predictor，再集成到MedSAM中联合优化。仅增加1.6M参数，推理开销可忽略不计。

Result: 在四个数据集上验证：BUSI（超声）Dice 0.89、FLARE22（CT）Dice 0.93、BRISC（MRI）Dice 0.88、LungSegDB（CT）Dice 0.98。方法在不同解剖结构和成像模态下均展现分割准确性和鲁棒性提升。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效解决点提示在医学图像分割中的空间模糊性问题，通过极小的参数增量实现了跨模态、跨解剖结构的泛化能力提升，验证了其在医学图像分割任务中的实用价值。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation in medical imaging is a critical yet challenging task due to data scarcity and high variability across modalities. While foundation models like the Segment Anything Model (SAM) show promise, they often struggle with medical images without specific adaptation. Moreover, point prompts, despite being the most natural form of user interaction, provide insufficient spatial context for reliable segmentation, particularly when target structures are irregular or poorly contrasted. In this paper, we propose an enhanced segmentation framework that integrates a lightweight Box Predictor module into the MedSAM architecture. The Box Predictor estimates an approximate bounding box from a single user click using localized image embedding features, providing spatial guidance that reduces the ambiguity of point prompts, while introducing only 1.6M additional parameters and negligible inference overhead. We introduce a two-stage training pipeline where the Box Predictor is trained independently before being integrated into MedSAM. To validate the generalization capability of our method, we conduct extensive evaluations on four diverse datasets (FLARE22, BRISC, BUSI, LungSegDB) spanning distinct imaging modalities, including CT, MRI, and Ultrasound. Our method improves segmentation accuracy and robustness across varied anatomical structures and imaging domains, achieving Dice scores of 0.89 (BUSI), 0.93 (FLARE22), 0.88 (BRISC), and 0.98 (LungSegDB). Code is available at https://github.com/Amirhosseinmovahedi/MedSAM-BoxPredictor

</details>


### [59] [ReConFuse: Reconstruction-Error Guided Semantic Fusion for AI-Generated Video Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04706)
*Xiaojing Chen,Xinyu Lu,Changtao Miao,Yunfeng Diao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ReConFuse框架，利用预训练WF-VAE的重构误差作为鉴别线索，结合多帧语义特征和Mamba时序建模，实现AI生成视频的通用检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成视频日益逼真，引发虚假信息、内容真实性等严重担忧，亟需可靠的AI视频检测方法，但现有方法在捕捉空间伪影、时序动态及泛化到新型生成模型方面仍面临挑战。

Method: 提出ReConFuse框架：首先利用预训练WF-VAE对输入视频进行重构并提取重构误差线索；然后将重构误差与多帧语义特征对齐；最后使用基于Mamba的模块对时序演化进行建模，实现视频级分类。

Result: 在多个生成器和多种评估设置下的实验表明，ReConFuse在检测效果和泛化能力上均表现优异。

Conclusion: 重构误差可作为区分真实与AI生成视频的有效鉴别线索，ReConFuse通过融合重构误差与语义特征并建模时序信息，实现了对AI生成视频的鲁棒检测。

Abstract: AI-generated videos are becoming increasingly realistic, raising serious concerns about misinformation, content authenticity, and media trust. Reliable AI-generated video detection is therefore essential for multimedia forensics, yet remains challenging due to the need to capture spatial artifacts, temporal dynamics, and generalize to evolving generative models. In this paper, we explore reconstruction error as a discriminative forensic cue for AI-generated video detection. By reconstructing input videos with a pretrained WF-VAE, we observe that real and generated videos exhibit distinguishable frame-wise reconstruction error patterns, suggesting that reconstruction errors can reveal their distributional discrepancies. However, extending reconstruction-based image detection to videos is non-trivial, since video reconstruction errors are temporally organized across frames and require semantic context for effective interpretation. To address these challenges, we propose ReConFuse, a reconstruction-guided semantic fusion framework for video-level AI-generated video detection. ReConFuse extracts reconstruction error cues from WF-VAE reconstructed videos, aligns them with multi-frame semantic features, and uses a Mamba-based module to model temporal evolution for video-level classification. Experiments across multiple generators and evaluation settings demonstrate the effectiveness and strong generalization ability of ReConFuse.

</details>


### [60] [Data Efficient Complex Feature Fusion Network For Hyperspectral Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04710)
*Maitreya Shelare,Atharva Satam,Poonam Sonar,Sneha Burnase*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种数据高效的注意力双支路复特征融合网络（DE-CFFN），通过因子分析降维、3D卷积滤波器数量逐层减半以及SE块特征增强，在保持与CFFN相当分类性能的同时大幅减小模型体积、内存消耗和推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始CFFN模型参数量大、计算成本高，难以满足高光谱图像实时分类的应用需求，因此需要设计一种数据高效的变体以降低复杂度同时保持性能。

Method: 1）保留RVNN和CVNN双支路结构；2）使用因子分析替代主成分分析进行降维以改进潜在特征表示；3）两个支路的3D卷积层滤波器数量逐层减半；4）双支路输出拼接后经过Squeeze and Excitation (SE)块增强联合特征表示。

Result: 在Pavia University和Salinas数据集上，DE-CFFN达到了与CFFN相当的分类性能，同时显著降低了模型大小、内存消耗和推理延迟。

Conclusion: DE-CFFN在保持分类精度的前提下实现了高效的模型压缩和加速，适合实时高光谱成像应用。

Abstract: This work presents a data-efficient variant of the Attention-Based Dual-Branch Complex Feature Fusion Network (CFFN) for hyperspectral image classification. The proposed model, termed DE-CFFN, retains the original two-stream structure: the Real-Valued Neural Network (RVNN) processes standard hyperspectral patches, while the Complex-Valued Neural Network (CVNN) handles their Fourier-transformed counterparts. The main contribution of this work lies in the feature extraction process and architectural enhancement. Factor Analysis is used for dimensionality reduction, offering improved latent feature representation over Principal Component Analysis. Additionally, both the RVNN and CVNN streams are structurally modified by successively halving the number of filters in the 3D convolutional layers to reduce complexity. The outputs of both branches are concatenated and passed through a Squeeze and Excitation (SE) block to enhance joint feature representation. Evaluated on the Pavia University and Salinas datasets, DE-CFFN achieves classification performance comparable to CFFN, while significantly reducing model size, memory consumption, and inference latency, making it suitable for real-time hyperspectral imaging applications.

</details>


### [61] [StrokeTimer: Robust Representation Learning for Ischemic Stroke Onset-Time Estimation from Non-contrast CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04722)
*Weiru Wang,Susanne G. H. Olthuis,Elizaveta Lavrova,Robert J. van Oostenbrugge,Charles B. L. M. Majoie,Wim H. van Zwam,Ruisheng Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出StrokeTimer，一种基于非增强CT的急性缺血性卒中发病时间自动估计框架，结合自监督解耦学习与能量引导对比学习，在大型多中心数据集上取得显著优于基线的性能（macro AUC 0.69），有望支持临床治疗决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺血性卒中的治疗决策高度依赖发病至干预的时间窗，但真实临床中发病时间常不明确。现有方法受限于非增强CT上早期缺血变化细微、发病时间类别分布不均衡以及不同中心/扫描仪间的数据异质性，亟需一种能应对这些挑战的自动化发病时间估计算法。

Method: 提出StrokeTimer框架，整合两个核心模块：(1) 自监督解耦学习（self-supervised disentanglement learning），用于捕获微弱的缺血模式特征；(2) 能量引导对比学习（energy-guided contrastive learning），用于处理长尾数据分布和采集变异。将发病时间分为三个临床相关窗口：<4.5h、4.5-6h、>6h。

Result: 在两个国家队列（MR CLEAN Registry和MR CLEAN LATE）的大型多中心NCCT数据集上，StrokeTimer的macro AUC达0.69，macro F1-score达0.57，相比最强基线提升近50%（p<0.005）。在具有挑战性的真实场景中，基线方法表现接近随机水平。模型解释突出了灰白质模糊和低密度区域，与已知放射学生物标志物一致。

Conclusion: StrokeTimer在真实临床场景下展示了可靠的发病时间估计能力，可望为急性缺血性卒中治疗决策提供影像学支持。代码已开源。

Abstract: Ischemic stroke is a major global disease. Treatment decisions are highly time-sensitive, as eligibility for reperfusion therapies relies on the interval between stroke onset and intervention. However, the true onset time is often uncertain in clinical practice, necessitating imaging-based assessment of tissue age as a surrogate marker. Early ischemic changes on routinely acquired non-contrast CT (NCCT) are often subtle, and real-world clinical datasets exhibit pronounced onset-time class imbalance and center-scanner-related heterogeneity. In this work, we propose StrokeTimer, a fully automated framework for onset-time estimation in acute ischemic stroke. StrokeTimer integrates self-supervised disentanglement learning with energy-guided contrastive learning to capture subtle ischemic patterns while addressing long-tailed data distributions under acquisition variability. Onset time is categorized into three clinically relevant windows: <4.5 h, 4.5-6 h, and >6 h. Experimental results on a large multi-center NCCT dataset from two national cohorts, MR CLEAN Registry and MR CLEAN LATE, show that StrokeTimer achieves a macro AUC of 0.69 and a macro F1-score of 0.57, improving the strongest baseline by nearly 50% (p < 0.005). In this realistic, challenging setting, representative baseline approaches exhibit near-chance macro performance. Model explanations further highlight subtle gray-white matter blurring and hypodense regions consistent with established radiological biomarkers. These findings demonstrate the potential of StrokeTimer to support treatment decision-making in acute ischemic stroke. Code is available at https://github.com/BrainVas/StrokeTimer.

</details>


### [62] [Physics-Informed Video Generation via Mixture-of-Experts Latent Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04737)
*Cong Wang,Hanxin Zhu,Jiayi Luo,Yonglin Tian,Xiaoqian Cheng,Peiyan Tu,Xin Jin,Long Chen,Zhibo Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PILA提出了一种物理信息潜空间对齐框架，通过向冻结的视频生成模型中注入物理结构化的潜在引导，在不破坏预训练视觉先验的前提下，显著提升了生成视频的物理合理性，在多个基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模视频生成模型虽在语义一致性和视觉质量上表现优异，但由于基于像素级拟合的动态建模无法自然适配真实世界的运动与交互规律，导致生成的视频在物理合理性方面存在持续缺陷。

Method: PILA框架包含三个核心步骤：(1) 锚定场估计，将冻结生成器的潜变量映射到由场代理槽组织的操作物理属性库中；(2) 基于物理类别的混合专家设计，通过标签先验掩码专家路由选择特定类别的算子专家，并由操作残差进行正则化；(3) 将精炼后的代理融合回物理属性库，解码为流匹配向量场的修正项，注入物理感知引导。

Result: 在Wan 2.1-1.3B上分阶段训练适配器，并直接迁移到Wan 2.2-14B上，PILA在VBench-2.0、VideoPhy-2和PhyGenBench上同时在视觉质量和物理合理性方面达到SOTA。

Conclusion: PILA通过物理学启发的潜空间对齐方法，有效解决了视频生成模型物理合理性不足的问题，且具有良好的可迁移性，为在保持视觉先验的同时注入物理约束提供了一条有效路径。

Abstract: Large-scale video generation models have made remarkable progress in semantic consistency and visual quality, producing videos that are increasingly coherent and visually convincing. Nevertheless, the dynamics induced by pixel-level fitting do not naturally accommodate the regularities that govern real-world motion and interaction, resulting in persistent shortcomings in physical plausibility. To address this limitation, we propose \textbf{PILA} (Physics-Informed Latent Alignment), a framework that injects physics-structured latent guidance into the frozen flow-matching dynamics of pretrained video models. Specifically, PILA first employs anchored field estimation to map frozen-generator latents into an operational physical attribute bank organized by field-proxy slots, using observable motion as a kinematic anchor for constructing less directly observed proxies. To handle the heterogeneity of real-world dynamics, PILA adopts a mixture-of-experts design over physical categories. Label-prior masked expert routing selects category-specific operator experts, whose refinements are regularized by operational residuals abstracted from physical relations. Finally, the refined proxies are fused into the physical attribute bank and decoded into a correction to the flow-matching vector field, injecting physics-aware guidance while preserving the visual prior of the pretrained backbone. With staged adapter training on Wan 2.1-1.3B and direct transfer of the learned adapter to Wan 2.2-14B, PILA achieves state-of-the-art results on VBench-2.0, VideoPhy-2, and PhyGenBench in both visual quality and benchmark-measured physical plausibility.

</details>


### [63] [Do Foundation Models See Biology? Evaluating Attention Coherence with Spatial Transcriptomics in Glioblastoma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04764)
*Dilakshan Srikanthan,Amoon Jamzad,Paul Wilson,Nooshin Maghsoodi,Robert Policelli,Gabor Fichtinger,John F. Rudan,Parvin Mousavi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于空间转录组学的正交、无假设框架，用于评估病理学基础模型注意力图的生物学相干性。在五类病理基础模型和ResNet50基线上的实验表明：注意力图捕获的是多基因转录程序（效应量d=0.329）而非单基因事件（d=0.055），不同编码器关注不同的生物学区室，且内部性能排名在外部验证中发生反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病理学基础模型的注意力图是否反映真实生物学机制尚不明确，而这一问题对临床信任和监管审批至关重要。现有研究多依赖定性显著图评估，缺乏客观定量方法，因此需要一个正交、无假设的评估框架来系统验证注意力图的生物学有效性。

Method: 构建基于空间转录组学的正交评估框架：1) 使用注意力多实例学习（attention-based MIL）在CPTAC队列上训练单任务和多任务模型，预测胶质母细胞瘤的5种分子改变；2) 在独立TCGA队列上进行外部验证；3) 利用18个样本的共配准Visium空间转录组数据，以87个转录特征为金标准，定量评估注意力图的生物学相干性。

Result: 1) 内部评估中无单一编码器在所有任务上占优，外部验证时内部性能排名发生反转；2) 注意力图对通路水平的富集效应量（Cohen's d=0.329）是基因水平（d=0.055）的约6倍，表明注意力捕获的是多基因转录程序；3) 空间平滑的注意力图不意味着生物学相干性；4) 不同编码器关注不同的生物学区室。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了客观、定量的病理基础模型生物学评估方法，推动该领域超越定性的显著图审查，为临床信任和监管审批提供支持。注意力图主要捕获多基因转录程序而非单个分子事件这一发现，对病理AI的可解释性研究具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: Whether attention maps from pathology foundation models capture genuine biology remains unknown, yet this question is critical for clinical trust and regulatory approval. We propose a spatial transcriptomics-based framework for orthogonal, hypothesis-free evaluation of attention and apply it to five pathology foundation models (CONCH v1.5, UNI v2, Virchow2, GigaPath, H-Optimus-1) and a ResNet50 baseline. Using attention-based multiple instance learning, we train single-task and multi-task models to predict five molecular alterations in glioblastoma on the CPTAC cohort, validate on an independent TCGA cohort, and evaluate biological coherence of attention maps against 87 transcriptional signatures using co-registered Visium spatial transcriptomics data from 18 samples. Internally, no single encoder dominates across all tasks, and external validation inverts internal performance rankings. Attention maps show a five-fold enrichment gradient from pathways (Cohen's d=0.329) to individual genes (d=0.055), indicating that attention captures emergent multi-gene transcriptional programs rather than individual molecular events. Spatially smooth attention maps do not imply biological coherence, and different encoders attend to distinct biological compartments. Our framework provides objective, quantitative assessment of what foundation models learn from histopathology, moving the field beyond qualitative saliency map review.

</details>


### [64] [Coarse-to-fine Hierarchical Architecture with Sequential Mamba for Brain Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04772)
*Hoang-Son Vo,Van-Hung Bui,Minh-Huy Mai-Duc,Tien-Dung Mai,Soo-Hyung Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CHASMBrain，一种用于图像到fMRI编码的分层双流Mamba框架，通过粗到细策略预测脑活动，在NSD数据集上达到0.429 Pearson相关，并揭示了补丁流与早期视觉皮层、CLS流与高级皮层区域的因果对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解深度视觉表征与人类视觉系统之间的关系是计算神经科学的基本挑战，现代视觉模型与人类视觉皮层层级组织的对应关系仍是一个开放问题。

Method: 提出CHASMBrain双流Mamba框架，显式分离全局语义token和局部空间补丁；采用粗到细策略：Stage1预测去噪的ROI级激活，Stage2使用Mamba-VAE细化为全voxel预测。

Result: 在NSD上达到Pearson相关0.429、MSE 0.261，优于所有基线；因果分支消融实验揭示不对称专业化：补丁流锁定早期视觉皮层（视网膜拓扑区），CLS流贡献高级区域的语义上下文；跨被试迁移实验表明模型捕获了共享的、与个体无关的视觉表征。

Conclusion: CHASMBrain不仅在预测性能上超越现有方法，还从因果角度验证了双流设计与视觉皮层功能组织的对应关系，且学到的表征具有跨被试泛化能力。

Abstract: Understanding the relationship between deep visual representations and the human visual system is a fundamental challenge in computational neuroscience. While modern vision models achieve strong performance in image recognition, their correspondence with the hierarchical organization of the human visual cortex remains an open question. In this study, we propose CHASMBrain, a novel hierarchical two-stage framework for image-to-fMRI encoding. Our architecture leverages a dual-stream Mamba design to explicitly separate and process global semantic tokens and local spatial patches, motivated by the functional organization of the visual cortex. A coarse-to-fine strategy is employed: Stage 1 predicts denoised ROI-level activations, while Stage 2 refines these coarse responses into full voxel-level predictions using a Mamba-VAE. Experiments on the Natural Scenes Dataset (NSD) demonstrate that our method achieves a Pearson correlation of 0.429 and an MSE of 0.261, outperforming all evaluated baselines including ridge regression and DINOv2 linear probes. Beyond predictive performance, causal branch-ablation experiments reveal an asymmetric specialization: the patch stream is specifically locked to early visual cortex (retinotopic regions), while the CLS stream contributes broader semantic context to higher-order areas -- a correspondence that holds causally, not merely correlationally. Cross-subject transfer experiments further show that the learned backbone generalizes across individuals with minimal per-subject adaptation, suggesting the model captures a shared, subject-agnostic visual representation.

</details>


### [65] [NextMotionQA: Benchmarking and Judging Human Motion Understanding with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04773)
*Yong Cao,Chuqiao Li,Xianghui Xie,Gerard Pons-Moll,Andreas Geiger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NextMotionQA是一个用于评估人类运动理解的综合基准，包含三个互补任务（多项选择问答、视频描述、细粒度错误修正），通过三个语义轴和三个复杂度层级系统化构建。对12种VLM的评估揭示了传统单任务评估无法发现的能力差距，同时发现VLM在粗粒度评估上与专家高度一致（κ=0.70），但在细粒度部件级判断上失效（κ=0.10）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人体运动理解基准存在语义粒度粗、难度区分度不足、标注质量有限和答案模糊性普遍等问题，无法诊断当前模型具体在哪些环节失败，因此亟需一个更全面、精细的评估基准来揭示模型真实能力边界。

Method: 利用视觉-语言模型（VLM）进行半自动化标注，并辅以专家验证；构建三项互补任务（多项选择问答、视频描述、细粒度错误修正）；沿三个核心语义轴（如空间、时间、交互等）系统组织，并划分为三个任务复杂度层级；对12种代表性VLM进行全面评估。

Result: VLM在粗粒度标准上与专家评分高度一致（Cohen's κ=0.70），但在细粒度部件级判断上表现极差（κ=0.10）；传统单任务评估无法发现的临界能力缺陷在NextMotionQA的多任务框架下被暴露。

Conclusion: NextMotionQA填补了现有运动理解基准的空白，能系统性诊断模型故障模式；VLM作为运动评估判断器的有效性仅在粗粒度任务上成立，在细粒度任务上存在根本性局限。

Abstract: Reliable evaluation of human motion understanding is fundamental to advancing embodied AI, robotics, and animation. However, existing benchmarks suffer from coarse semantic granularity, undifferentiated difficulty, limited annotation quality, and pervasive answer ambiguity, leaving them unable to diagnose where current models fail. To bridge this gap, we introduce NextMotionQA, a comprehensive benchmark that leverages vision-language models (VLMs) for semi-automated, expert-verified dataset. NextMotionQA features three complementary tasks: multiple-choice question answering, video captioning, and fine-grained error correction. Each task is systematically structured across three core semantic axes and stratified into three task complexity levels. Our extensive evaluation of twelve representative VLMs uncovers critical capability gaps and weakness that remain invisible under conventional, single-task evaluations. In a complementary direction, recent work has begun using VLMs as judges for text-to-motion evaluation; we ask whether they show the same degradation under harder tasks. We find that VLMs align strongly with expert ratings on coarse criteria (Cohen's κ=0.70) but break down on fine-grained, part-level judgment (κ=0.10), validating the paradigm in its strong regime while clarifying its limits.

</details>


### [66] [Z-FLoc: Zero-Shot Floorplan Localization via Geometric Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04788)
*Ayumi Umemura,Toshinori Kuwahara,Marc Pollefeys,Daniel Barath*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种零样本的楼层平面定位方法，通过从单目3D重建的鸟瞰图中提取线、圆等几何基元，与楼层平面匹配，无需重新训练即可在新环境中实现鲁棒的视觉定位，性能优于现有学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于数据驱动的楼层平面定位方法需要大量训练数据和环境特定的重新训练，限制了实际部署。本文旨在提出一种无需重新训练即可泛化到新环境的零样本定位方法。

Method: 从单目3D重建的鸟瞰图（BEV）投影中提取线、圆等几何基元，利用这些基元作为外观不变的结构约束，通过专用的最小求解器在鲁棒估计框架内与楼层平面进行匹配。

Result: 在模拟和真实数据集上，该方法在未见过的环境中优于当前最先进的基于学习方法，且所有实验使用同一组超参数。

Conclusion: 利用人造环境中普遍存在的几何基元（线、圆）作为结构约束，能够在零样本条件下实现跨场景的楼层平面定位，具有强泛化能力。

Abstract: Visual localization -- estimating a camera pose within a pre-existing map -- is a fundamental problem in computer vision.
  Floorplans are an attractive map representation: they are readily available for most buildings, compact, and inherently invariant to visual appearance changes.
  However, bridging the severe domain gap between camera observations and floorplan geometry remains challenging.
  Existing methods address this gap through data-driven learning, yet they require large-scale training data and environment-specific retraining, limiting their practical deployment.
  We propose a zero-shot floorplan localization method that generalizes to novel environments without any retraining.
  Our key insight is that dominant geometric primitives -- lines and circles -- are ubiquitous in human-made environments and provide appearance-invariant structural constraints.
  We extract these primitives from a bird's-eye-view (BEV) projection of monocular 3D reconstructions and match them to the floorplan via dedicated minimal solvers within a robust estimation framework.
  Experiments on both simulated and real-world datasets show that our approach outperforms state-of-the-art learning-based methods on unseen environments, while using a single fixed set of hyperparameters across all experiments.
  The source code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [67] [A Pathology Foundation Model for Gastric Cancer with Real-World Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04792)
*Ling Liang,Jiabo Ma,Zhengyu Zhang,Fengtao Zhou,Yingxue Xu,Yihui Wang,Cheng Jin,Zhengrui Guo,On Ki Tang,Zhijian Cen,Zhen Wang,Qi Xie,Chengyu Lu,Chenglong Zhao,Feifei Wang,Yu Cai,Hongyi Wang,Jing Zhang,Yaping Ye,Shijun Sun,Shenglei Li,Yu Wang,Zhenhui Li,Ronald Cheong Kin Chan,Xiuming Zhang,Zhe Wang,Hao Chen,Li Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GRACE是一个胃癌专用基础模型，基于48,364张HE染色全切片图像开发，在28项临床任务上优于通用病理基础模型（宏AUC 0.9188），并通过安全门控标准（可分流69.6%恶性诊断病例和46.8%MMR-IHC随访请求）、随机交叉读者研究（AI辅助下诊断准确率从82.0%提升至89.9%，OR=1.987，诊断时间减少14.9%，置信度提升9.0%）等严格证据链验证了其转化价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胃癌的组织学和分子异质性使得诊断和风险分层困难，通用病理基础模型在胃癌细粒度临床任务上表现停滞，且缺乏前瞻性验证和临床读者研究。

Method: 从多中心胃癌病理数据集（48,364张HE染色全切片图像，37,493例患者）开发GRACE模型；在28项临床任务上评估；通过安全门控标准（100% NPV和100% PPV）、随机交叉读者研究（病理学家-AI协作）及校准到非劣效于高级病理医生后的三重分流机制验证转化价值。

Result: GRACE在28项任务上宏AUC 0.9188，癌前病变诊断0.9322，肿瘤组织学评估0.9119，分子分型0.8682；安全门控可分流69.6%恶性诊断和46.8%MMR-IHC随访；AI辅助使诊断准确率从82.0%提升至89.9%（OR=1.987），敏感性和特异性同时提升，诊断时间减少14.9%，置信度提升9.0%，评分者间一致性显著改善；校准后可分流60.7%萎缩和82.7%肠化生病例。

Conclusion: GRACE作为胃癌专用基础模型，在多项临床任务上显著优于通用PFM，并通过严格的临床转化验证链条（安全门控、读者研究、非劣效校准）证明了其实际临床决策支持价值，有望改善胃癌诊疗流程。

Abstract: Gastric cancer remains a major cause of cancer mortality, yet its histological and molecular heterogeneity complicates diagnosis and risk stratification. General-purpose pathology foundation models (PFMs) often plateau on fine-grained endpoints central to gastric cancer care, and few have undergone rigorous prospective validation or clinical reader studies. We present GRACE, a Gastric-specific foundation model for Real-world Assessment and Clinical dEcision support. GRACE was developed from multicenter gastric pathology datasets totaling 48,364 primarily HE-stained whole-slide images from 37,493 patients. When evaluated on 28 clinically relevant tasks, GRACE consistently outperformed representative pancancer PFMs, achieving a macro-AUC of 0.9188, with strong performance for precancerous lesion diagnosis (macro-AUC 0.9322), tumor histopathological assessment (macro-AUC 0.9119), molecular profiling (macro-AUC 0.8682), and prognostic prediction. Beyond benchmarking, GRACE's translational value was substantiated through a rigorous evidence chain. Under safety-gated criteria requiring 100% NPV for rule-out and 100% PPV for rule-in, GRACE streamlined review for up to 69.6% of malignancy-diagnosis cases and triaged 46.8% of MMR-IHC follow-up requests. This translational feasibility was further strengthened by a randomized crossover reader study of pathologist-AI collaboration. With GRACE assistance, diagnostic accuracy improved from 82.0% to 89.9%, yielding nearly twofold higher adjusted odds of a correct diagnosis (OR 1.987) alongside concurrent gains in sensitivity and specificity. AI assistance also reduced diagnostic time by 14.9%, elevated diagnostic confidence by 9.0%, and markedly improved inter-rater agreement. When calibrated to maintain non-inferior performance to senior pathologists, the AI-assisted workflow could triage 60.7% of atrophy and 82.7% of intestinal metaplasia cases.

</details>


### [68] [Crafting Your Evolving Dreams: Concept-Incremental Versatile Customization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04797)
*Jiahua Dong,Wenqi Liang,Hongliu Li,Yang Cong,Duzhen Zhang,Hanbin Zhao,Henghui Ding,Yulun Zhang,Salman Khan,Fahad Shahbaz Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CCDM框架，通过属性解耦LoRA、相关性引导聚合策略和可控区域上下文合成策略，解决了扩散模型在增量学习个性化概念时的灾难性遗忘和概念忽视问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有定制化扩散模型假设用户的概念集合是静态不可增长的，且无法增量学习新概念，存在严重的灾难性遗忘和概念忽视问题。

Method: 1) 设计属性解耦LoRA(AD-LoRA)模块保留每个任务的特有属性；2) 提出相关性引导的AD-LoRA聚合策略利用任务间有益相关性增强持续学习；3) 提出可控区域上下文合成策略，通过语义独立性和平滑边界转换实现多概念合成。

Result: 实验结果表明CCDM在持续定制化任务上显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: CCDM成功实现了概念增量的多功能定制，有效缓解了灾难性遗忘和概念忽视，为扩散模型的持续个性化学习提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Custom diffusion models (CDMs) have garnered significant interest owing to their remarkable capacity for generating personalized concepts. However, the majority of CDMs unrealistically presume that the user's collection of personalized concepts is static and incapable of incremental growth over time. Furthermore, they exhibit significant catastrophic forgetting and concept neglect of previously learned concepts when incrementally learning a sequence of new ones. To resolve the above challenges, we develop a novel Continually Customizable Diffusion Model (CCDM), enabling users to perform concept-incremental versatile customization. Specifically, we design an attribute-decoupled LoRA (AD-LoRA) module and a relevance-guided AD-LoRA aggregation strategy to mitigate catastrophic forgetting. They can preserve concept-specific attributes of each task and leverage beneficial inter-task correlations to enhance the continual learning of new customization tasks. Additionally, to address the challenge of concept neglect, we propose a controllable regional context synthesis strategy that performs multi-concept composition in alignment with user-provided conditions. This strategy enhances the overall consistency in multi-concept synthesis by guaranteeing semantic independence between user-defined regions and their smooth boundary transitions. Experiments show our CCDM exhibits significant improvements over baseline methods.

</details>


### [69] [Fast Cubical Persistent Homology on 2D and 3D Images via Union-Find, Pruning, and Lookup Tables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04801)
*Titouan Le Breton,Karol Szustakowski,Marie Piraud*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Flash Cubical，一种基于V-滤波的立方体持久性高效计算方法，利用立方体复形的性质、边剪枝和查找表技术，在2D和3D图像上实现了当前最优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有立方体持久性计算方法效率不足，尤其在时间和内存成本方面有待改进，需要一种更高效的实现方案。

Method: 三个核心创新：(1)利用立方体复形的性质，通过并查集和对偶性计算最高维的持久性；(2)对特定边进行剪枝，加速并查集实现；(3)利用查找表预计算局部信息，避免运行时重复计算。

Result: 在V-滤波立方体复形上实现了当前最高效的持久性计算，时间和内存成本均达到最优。

Conclusion: 所提方法不仅适用于V-滤波，还能自然推广到T-滤波及其他复形结构，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: We present Flash Cubical, a highly efficient computation of cubical persistence on a V-filtration for 2D and 3D images over $\mathbb{F}_2$. The implementation is built around three core ideas. First, cubical complexes satisfy properties that allow for the computation of persistence of the highest dimension via union-find and duality. Second, pruning of certain edges allows for a fast and efficient implementation of union-find. Third, the use of a lookup table, which exploits the regularity of cubical complexes to pre-compute local information. This avoids the need to compute local information at run time. To the best of our knowledge, this is the most efficient implementation of cubical persistence with a V-filtration, both in terms of time and memory costs. Although the paper focuses on persistence for V-filtration cubical complexes, the underlying ideas generalise naturally to T-filtrations on cubical complexes and suggest promising directions for other complexes.

</details>


### [70] [NoRA: Evaluating Grounded Reasonableness in Visual First-person Normative Action Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04806)
*Sichao Li,Sai Ma,Daniel Kilov,Secil Yanik Guyot,Zhuang Li,Seth Lazar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NoRA是一个视觉第一人称视频基准，要求模型生成候选下一步动作并通过显式的事实-理由-动作支持图进行论证。包含1,420个视频片段，评估发现当前VLM虽能恢复合理动作和相关场景事实，但在构建完整合理动作空间和将选定动作绑定到正确局部支持方面存在困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么仅在文本中评估规范性判断，要么将判断简化为从固定候选动作集中选择，不足以评估实际部署中的智能体行为。智能体在实践中不会被提供选项菜单，必须从零开始识别合理动作，并基于可见事实和可检查的理由进行论证。

Method: 构建包含1,420个标注视频片段的NoRA基准（HumanGold-190和LLMSilver-1230），要求模型生成候选下一步动作并通过事实-理由-动作支持图进行论证。通过动作对齐、事实基础和支持绑定三个维度进行评估，聚合为单一的基础合理性分数，在直接、深思和结构化三种提示范式下评估12个多模态系统。

Result: 当前VLM经常能恢复合理动作和相关场景事实，但始终难以构建完整的合理动作空间，并将选定的动作绑定到正确的局部支持上。

Conclusion: NoRA使这一差距可测量，将评估问题从模型能否选择一个动作转变为模型能否基于正确的可见理由为适当的动作进行论证。

Abstract: LLMs and agentic systems are increasingly deployed in social environments, making normative competence critical for safe and appropriate behavior. However, existing approaches either assess normative judgment in text alone or reduce it to choosing among a fixed set of candidate actions. We argue both are insufficient. In practice, agents are never handed a menu of options; they must identify a reasonable action from scratch, grounded in visible facts and supported by inspectable reasons. We introduce NoRA, a visual first-person video benchmark that requires models to generate candidate next actions and justify each through an explicit fact-reason-action support graph. The benchmark comprises 1,420 annotated video clips, including HumanGold-190 and LLMSilver-1230 splits. Each instance is evaluated through action alignment, factual grounding, and support binding, aggregated into a single grounded reasonableness score. We benchmark 12 multimodal systems under direct, deliberate, and structured prompting regimes, finding that current VLMs frequently recover plausible actions and relevant scene facts, but consistently struggle to construct the full reasonable action space and bind selected actions to the correct local support. NoRA makes this gap measurable, shifting the evaluation question from whether a model can pick an action to whether it can justify an appropriate action for the right visible reasons.

</details>


### [71] [Dream.exe: Can Video Generation Models Dream Executable Robot Manipulation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04811)
*Rui Zhao,Kaiming Yang,Jifeng Zhu,Siyang Chen,Ziqi Wang,Weijia Wu,Kevin Qinghong Lin,Heng Wang,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Dream.exe评估框架，通过视频生成→机器人轨迹→物理仿真执行的三阶段流水线，衡量视频生成模型对物理世界的理解能力。在101个操作任务上评估8个模型发现：部分模型已具备可测量的物理知识，但视觉质量与可执行性严重脱节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型评价仅停留在视觉质量层面，无法衡量模型是否真正理解物理世界规律。作者希望以机器人操作任务为具体窗口，通过"生成视频能否转化为可执行的机器人动作"来检验模型对物理世界的理解程度。

Method: 提出Dream.exe框架，包含三阶段流水线：1) 输入场景图像+任务描述，生成操作视频；2) 将生成的运动转化为机器人轨迹；3) 在物理仿真器中执行轨迹，获得可执行性信号。涵盖101个手工操作任务，分三个物理复杂度等级，从视觉质量、轨迹保真度、执行成功率三个维度评估8个模型（闭源/开源/机器人专用）。

Result: 多个模型取得了可测量的执行成功率，表明从互联网数据学习到的生成先验已编码了有意义的物理知识。但视觉质量与可执行性之间呈弱相关，说明标准视觉评估无法捕捉模型在物理理解维度的能力差距。

Conclusion: 视频生成模型已在某种程度上内化了物理规律，但视觉质量的提升并不等价于物理理解能力的增强，需要引入类似Dream.exe的行为级评估来全面衡量模型对物理世界的建模能力。

Abstract: Video generation models have made impressive strides in synthesizing visually compelling content, yet their outputs remain confined to the virtual domain. A natural question follows: how well do these models reflect the physical world when their generated videos leave the screen and enter reality? We propose robotic manipulation as a concrete, measurable window onto this question: if a model has truly internalized physical laws, the motion it depicts should translate into executable robot behavior. We introduce Dream.exe, an evaluation framework that operationalizes this criterion through a video-to-execution pipeline. Given a scene image and a task description, Dream.exe synthesizes a manipulation video, converts the generated motion into robot trajectories, and executes them in a physics simulator, yielding a grounding signal that purely visual metrics cannot offer. Using this pipeline, we evaluate 8 models spanning frontier closed-source generators, open-source generators, and robot-specific models. Our benchmark covers 101 manually curated manipulation tasks at three levels of physical complexity, measured across visual quality, trajectory fidelity, and execution success. Encouragingly, several models achieve measurable execution success, suggesting that generative priors learned from internet-scale data already encode meaningful physical knowledge. Yet visual quality proves a poor predictor of executability, exposing a dimension of model capability that standard visual evaluations do not capture. Dream.exe will be open-sourced at https://github.com/showlab/Dream.exe.

</details>


### [72] [OA-CutMix: Correcting the Label Bias of CutMix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04820)
*Tobias Christian Nauen,Stanislav Frolov,Federico Raue,Brian B. Moser,Andreas Dengel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OA-CutMix通过基于分割掩码的标签分配修正了CutMix中区域标签与语义贡献不匹配的偏差，在不改变图像混合过程的情况下，在多个架构和数据集上超越10余种混合方法，尤其显著提升小物体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
